## 正本（オラクル）

- 設計: /home/y-saito/Developments/hareruya-design-docs/design_impl_drift_report/design_audit/0208/sheets/<シートID>.txt
  （正本HTML設計書 0208 のシート本文を機械抽出したもの。**設計の正はここだけ**）
  レイアウト図は /home/y-saito/Developments/hareruya-design-docs/design_impl_drift_report/design_audit/0208/images/<シートID>_img*.png
- 実装: /home/y-saito/Developments/ec-cube-enterprise （ee）。**実装の正はここだけ**

## 正本でないもの

## 探してほしいもの（優先順）

### 1. ハルシネーション（最優先）

引用も実装参照も実在するのに、**主張だけが事実と違う**もの。機械検査では捕まりません。
すでに機械で次は確認済みなので、ここを繰り返す必要はありません。

- 設計根拠_引用が正本シート本文に逐語で実在すること（全件すべて実在）
- 実装参照が ee 内に実在するファイル:行であること（全件すべて実在）

疑ってほしいのは次です。

- 「〜していない」と書いてあるが、実は**別の場所でやっている**
  （親クラス・トレイト・イベントリスナ・サブスクライバ・ミドルウェア・
   フォームの制約・バリデータ・DBの制約・Doctrineのライフサイクル）
- 「実装が無い」と書いてあるが、**探し方が悪くて見つけられていない**だけ
- 実装の分岐の読み違い（条件の向き・early return・例外経路）
- 行番号がずれていて、引用した行が主張と関係ない

### 2. 過剰指摘

次は規約で対象外です。指摘になっていたら挙げてください。

- 実装手段の差（通信方式・認可設定・内部配線・クラス名・呼び出し順・join の種類）
- 応答にも画面にも出ない内部挙動
- 現行仕様に書かれた現行ソースの不具合・不用意な副作用（踏襲対象ではない）。
  ただし機能として定義された挙動（警告表示・並び順・据え置き時間等）は要求として扱う
- 現行仕様とリニューアル後の仕様が食い違う箇所は**後者が優先**。現行仕様側は対象外
- 廃止・フェーズ2と正本が宣言しているもの
- 見出し・表の見出し行・区分行・前置き・運用想定・背景説明
- 設計根拠_引用が**仕様を述べていない**もの（項目名・備考・表のヘッダの読み違い）

### 3. 重複

根本原因キーが違っても、実装実態が同じ欠陥を指していれば重複です。
機械で「判定＋実装実態の完全一致」は確認済みです。**文言が違う重複**を探してください。

### 4. 重要度の過大

P1/P2/P3 が業務影響と迂回可否に見合っているか。

## 指摘対象外（これを指摘しても却下します）

- 設計書の書き方・粒度・文体
- 指摘が**少なすぎる**という主張（過小報告の指摘は別途人が見ます）
- 正本シート本文に無いことを根拠にした「設計にこう書くべき」という主張
- MATCHED・OUT_OF_SCOPE・UNVERIFIABLE と判定したものの妥当性
  （成果物には載っていません。本レビューの範囲外）

## 出力形式

指摘が無ければ `NONE` の1語だけ。

指摘ごとに次を書いてください。

```
要求ID:
判定: ハルシネーション | 過剰指摘 | 重複 | 重要度過大
主張: 成果物が何と言っているか
実際: 実装の file:line を必ず示す。何行目に何と書いてあるか
修正案: 取り下げ / 判定をこう変える / 重要度をこうする / どの要求IDへ畳む
```

**重要度過大と判断したときは、修正案に「P3へ変更する」のように正しい重要度を必ず書いてください。**
書かないと修正が適用されず、重要度は過大なまま残ります。
**重複と判断したときは、修正案に畳む先の要求ID（sheet-N-RNNN）を必ず書いてください。**

# Design Impl Drift Audit

## Purpose

Use this skill to audit functional differences between HTML design specifications and an implementation repository, with the design docs treated as authoritative. Produce or continue a report that lists concrete differences with design locations, implementation locations, confidence, severity, and impact.

## Repository Discovery

1. Find the design-doc directory and implementation directory from the user request. If omitted, infer from nearby names such as `*-design-docs` and application repos.
2. Look for an existing report before starting new work. Prefer directories named `design_impl_drift_report`, existing `findings.json`, per-function `findings/*.json`, and `index.html`.
3. Count total target specs and audited specs so the report clearly states scope and remaining work.
4. Preserve existing findings. Append or refine; do not overwrite unrelated prior work.

## Audit Workflow

For each target HTML spec:

1. Identify the feature ID, route/action name, UI template, CSV/batch/API behavior, and acceptance rules from the HTML design spec.
2. Extract a requirement inventory from the HTML spec before looking at code. This inventory must include not only route/screen names, but every concrete requirement in 処理概要, 図形・テキストボックス, 項目表, 表示メッセージ, 業務ルール・計算, 入力項目, エッジケース, データ整合性, API/バッチ結果, 入出力, DB操作, バリデーション, 権限・認可, 画面遷移, エラー処理, ログ・監査, セッション, Cookie, and 排他制御. Treat bullets such as "押下時", "場合", "登録/更新/保存", "遷移", "保持", "表示/非表示", "表示切り替え", "エリアを表示", "誘導", "エラー", "判定", "連携", time limits, thresholds, and retry/idempotency rules as auditable requirements. 秒後/分後, `画面を表示してから`, 自動遷移, タイマー, and store/kiosk/account-specific forced redirects are material requirements even if the route and template already exist.
3. For every extracted requirement, create or mentally maintain a `requirementTrace` row with: `requirementId`, `designRef`, `designRequirement`, `implementationSearchTerms`, `implementationRefs`, `implementationActual`, `traceVerdict` (`MATCHED|DRIFT|NOT_FOUND|UNCERTAIN|PHASE2_EXCLUDED`), and `traceReason`. A feature cannot be marked no-drift until all material requirements have a trace verdict other than `NOT_FOUND` or unresolved `UNCERTAIN`.
4. Search the implementation with `rg` across likely files: controllers, services, repositories, form types, entities, migrations, Twig/templates, commands/batches, route definitions, JavaScript, CSV exporters/importers, and PDF/print services. Search by business terms from the requirement, route names, translation keys, DB columns, state names, constants, statuses, time values, thresholds, and side-effect verbs. For timer or auto-navigation requirements, explicitly search client and server paths for `setTimeout`, `setInterval`, millisecond/second constants such as `60000` and `60 * 1000`, `location.href`, `location.assign`, `location.replace`, redirect routes, and the condition that limits the behavior. For a negative result, also search nearby implemented flows so the report can explain why they do not satisfy the design.
5. Compare behavior and user-facing text, not just names. Check input fields, validation, defaults, search conditions, session persistence, buttons/actions, labels, button text, messages, translation keys/locales, permissions, status transitions, CSV columns, sort/order rules, scheduled timing, timeouts/expiration, thresholds, client-side timers, forced auto-navigation, side effects, idempotency, external integrations, conditional display toggles, alternate UI areas, and error handling.
6. Do not treat "route/controller/template exists" as sufficient. Existence only satisfies the route/screen requirement. It does not satisfy individual rules inside the screen, such as timeout handling, cart retention, validation branches, status transitions, DB writes, logging prohibitions, conditional hide/show behavior, alternative contact/help areas, or external API retry behavior.
7. For conditional display requirements, audit each side of the condition separately. If the HTML says "Aの場合はXを非表示、Yを表示 / A以外はXを表示、Yを非表示", record trace rows for the condition detector, X visibility, Y visibility, and the inverse condition. A nearby notice or route is not enough unless the cited template/JS actually renders the required area under the required condition.
8. For labels, button text, messages, and translation keys, do a dedicated near-match pass. Extract user-visible Japanese text from the HTML item/message tables and compare it with implementation locale values and Twig/Form usage. Treat punctuation, okurigana, missing characters, long-vowel marks, wording changes such as `商品を追加` vs `商品の追加`, and business-term changes such as `返金` vs `送金` as audit targets.
9. A text drift finding is complete only when it cites both the translation/source value and the display path that uses it, such as `messages.ja.yaml` plus the `FormType` label or Twig `|trans` call. If the implementation value exists but no display path is verified, mark it `UNCERTAIN` instead of presenting it as confirmed.
10. Do not stamp all functions as if every standard aspect was fully checked. Separate "standard audit target aspects" from actual evidence. For each finding, list the aspects actually supported by evidence; for no-drift functions, include a requirement trace summary naming the concrete HTML requirements checked and implementation refs searched.
11. Record only actionable drift. If evidence is incomplete, mark the finding `UNCERTAIN` and explain what remains ambiguous.
12. For every drift finding, split the result into implementation-ready fields: `designExpectation`, `implementationActual`, `comparisonRows`, `comparisonSummary`, `mismatchReason`, `impact`, `fixTarget`, `requiredChange`, `implementationRefs`, and, when available, short design/implementation snippets from the cited lines. Do not rely on a generic prose paragraph alone.
13. Each `comparisonRows` entry must answer all four questions explicitly: comparison item, exact design value/requirement, exact implementation value/behavior, and why those two do not match. If any of those four cells would be vague, continue investigating before recording the finding.
14. Treat phase labels such as Ph2 as auxiliary context. They can be stored, but the main report must not use phase status as a substitute for concrete behavioral differences and exact implementation locations.
15. If the HTML design spec explicitly says the whole function or scoped subfeature is `フェーズ2で実装する`, `Ph2で対応`, or `Ph1では実装しない`, do not report absence of that planned Phase 2 work as drift or as a clear implementation gap. You may keep a secondary audit note that non-implementation was confirmed, but exclude it from finding counts and implementation-gap counts. Only keep a finding when there is a concrete non-phase mismatch, such as an implemented Phase 1 path behaving differently from the design or user-facing text differing.
16. When no drift is found, add the function to the audited set with an empty findings list only after the requirement trace gate passes. If material HTML requirements are untraced, do not mark `AUDITED_NO_DRIFT_FOUND`; mark the function as requiring review or add `UNCERTAIN` findings with the unresolved requirements.
17. Do not let one confirmed finding hide other requirements in the same function. A function with `AUDITED_WITH_FINDINGS` can still have untraced material requirements; keep those requirements visible in the trace gate until each one is converted to a confirmed finding, an uncertain finding, a matched trace row, or a documented phase/design non-implementation exclusion.
18. For explicitly registered critical requirements, the harness should carry a missing-finding template or an equivalent escalation rule. If the trace is not covered by an existing finding, promote it into findings automatically or fail the quality gate loudly. Do not rely on a hand-maintained `ADDITIONAL_FINDINGS` list as the only path for user-reported or high-risk missing behavior such as timeouts, cart retention, one-minute/30-minute limits, store-account auto redirects, conditional UI areas, status transitions, external calls, or destructive side effects.
19. The HTML report must not expose broad source-signal candidates as if they were implementation gaps. A requirement-level list in the main report must be limited to confirmed or explicitly uncertain actionable drift derived from findings, such as `NOT_FOUND` or `DRIFT` requirement traces and concrete comparison rows. If a source-signal scan is used for triage, keep it out of the main HTML report unless each row has been verified against implementation source and written as a concrete finding.
20. Do not hide time-based or automatic-navigation requirements merely because they are not confirmed findings yet. Requirements involving `画面を表示してから`, seconds/minutes after display, auto redirect/navigation, or timeout-to-cart/top behavior should appear in a separate focused untraced-requirement audit queue when they remain uncovered. This queue is not a confirmed defect count; it exists so these high-risk requirements are reviewed against Controller/Twig/JavaScript/Service source instead of being buried among broad requirement-trace failures.

## Function-Level Implementation Gap Audit

For every HTML spec sheet, maintain a function-level implementation audit even when no drift finding is confirmed yet.

- Extract implementation search terms from the HTML design: route names, URL paths, translation keys, class/controller/service names, batch/API/CSV names, and high-signal business terms from the sheet title and requirement text.
- Search implementation source by function across Controller, Form, Service, Repository, Entity, Migration, Twig, Command/Batch, CSV, PDF/print, API/webhook, locale/messages, JavaScript, and configuration files.
- Store a `functionImplementationAudit` object with `verdict`, `designDerivedTerms`, `candidateRefs`, `candidateCount`, `strongSignalCount`, `searchedAreas`, and `auditNote`.
- Use separate verdicts:
  - `CONFIRMED_IMPLEMENTATION_GAP`: a concrete drift finding already proves missing implementation.
  - `NO_IMPLEMENTATION_ENTRY_FOUND`: no entry candidate was found from design-derived terms; treat as an additional-review candidate, not an automatic confirmed defect.
  - `IMPLEMENTATION_ENTRY_FOUND`: strong source evidence such as route, class, translation key, or path was found.
  - `IMPLEMENTATION_CANDIDATE_FOUND`: weak business-term candidates were found but the entry point is not proven.
  - `PHASE2_NONIMPLEMENTATION_EXPECTED`: HTML design explicitly scopes non-implementation to Phase 2/Ph1 exclusion.
  - `DESIGN_NONIMPLEMENTATION_EXPECTED`: HTML design explicitly says the function or scoped behavior is not implemented by code, such as `実装しない`, `実装不要`, or infrastructure/external-service configuration replacing application code.
- Use `DESIGN_NONIMPLEMENTATION_EXPECTED` as the function-level verdict only when the non-implementation statement applies to the whole function or the implementation entry itself, for example an external WordPress server, Docker image cron, Aurora parameter group, or a bare whole-sheet `実装しない`. If the text only excludes a scoped subfeature, UI alert, HTML hint, logging detail, partial Phase 2 item, or internal job, keep the function's source-entry verdict and mention the scoped exclusion only in the trace/audit note.
- The report must show both confirmed implementation-gap findings and function-level no-entry candidates. Do not merge these counts: implementers need confirmed defects for fixing and no-entry candidates for review planning.
- A function-level candidate is not enough to mark the detailed requirements as implemented. Requirement trace still controls no-drift status.
- Do not set report metadata such as `auditComplete` or narrative notes to "complete" merely because every sheet has a function-level implementation audit. If any material requirement trace remains unresolved, state that all sheets have function-entry coverage but requirement-level audit is incomplete.
- For infrastructure-oriented sheets such as Step Functions, Lambda, WAF/S3 jobs, cron configured outside the application, or managed database settings, distinguish "not found in the requested implementation repository" from "not implemented anywhere". A confirmed implementation-gap finding may be scoped to the audited repository, but the report must say when external infrastructure repositories or cloud configuration were not checked.

## Requirement Trace Gate

Before marking a function no-drift, verify the following:

- Every material HTML requirement has a trace row. Material means it changes behavior, data, display, validation, permission, transition, side effect, external communication, schedule, retry/idempotency, logging/audit, CSV/API/PDF/batch I/O, or user-visible text.
- Each trace row includes exact design text or a tight paraphrase plus line/anchor reference, not only the sheet title.
- Each matched row cites the implementation path and line(s) that actually execute or render the behavior. A nearby class, route, or template is not enough unless the cited code contains the relevant condition/value/side effect.
- Each missing row is converted to a confirmed or uncertain finding. Use confirmed only after searching the direct path and nearby alternative implementations.
- Missing rows remain failures even if the function already has other findings. The gate is requirement-scoped, not function-scoped.
- If an explicit critical requirement has a configured missing-finding template, regenerate the report from the harness and verify the promoted finding appears in both JSON and HTML with design value, implementation behavior, mismatch reason, fix target, and search evidence.
- If a missing row does not yet have enough evidence for a confirmed or uncertain actionable finding, do not count it as an implementation gap and do not put it in the main HTML fix list. Keep the function in an incomplete requirement-trace state, and promote the row only after implementation source has been checked enough to state the exact design requirement, exact implementation behavior, and mismatch reason.
- A generic `auditEvidence` string is not a substitute for requirement traces.
- Negative searches must list the searched terms/areas and why nearby hits do not satisfy the design.

## Finding Format

Store findings in a machine-readable JSON structure. Use this shape for each finding:

```json
{
  "id": "stable-feature-id-001",
  "functionId": "m04-01_admin_stock_stock_search_list",
  "functionName": "在庫一覧",
  "severity": "high|med|low",
  "dimension": "①機能/ルート未実装",
  "confidence": "CONFIRMED|UNCERTAIN",
  "design": {
    "file": "path/to/spec.html",
    "lines": "215-220",
    "summary": "設計書に書かれている仕様"
  },
  "implementation": {
    "file": "path/to/source.php or 不在",
    "lines": "337-344",
    "summary": "実装側の状態"
  },
  "difference": "設計と実装の具体的な差分",
  "comparisonRows": [
    {
      "item": "比較項目名",
      "design": "設計書の値・要求。列名、HTTPメソッド、パス、保存先、表示文言、条件、状態遷移などを具体値で書く",
      "implementation": "実装の値・挙動。実際の列名、メソッド、パス、保存先、文言、条件、処理順を具体値で書く",
      "mismatch": "なぜ一致しないのか。実装者が直すべき差分を一文で書く"
    }
  ],
  "requirementTrace": [
    {
      "requirementId": "stable-requirement-id",
      "designRef": "path/to/spec.html:123",
      "designRequirement": "HTML設計書の個別要求",
      "implementationSearchTerms": ["検索語", "route_name", "methodName"],
      "implementationRefs": ["src/...php:123"],
      "implementationActual": "実装で確認できた挙動、または不在判定",
      "traceVerdict": "MATCHED|DRIFT|NOT_FOUND|UNCERTAIN|PHASE2_EXCLUDED",
      "traceReason": "なぜその判定か"
    }
  ],
  "comparisonSummary": "設計はX、実装はY、したがってZが違う",
  "mismatchReason": "乖離と判断した具体的な理由",
  "impact": "ユーザー・業務・データへの影響",
  "fixTarget": "修正対象のファイル/ルート/サービス/不在箇所",
  "requiredChange": "設計に合わせる場合に必要な修正",
  "implementationRefs": ["src/...php:123"],
  "implementationSnippets": ["123: 実装コードの短い断片"],
  "evidence": ["短い根拠。ファイル名と行番号を含める"]
}
```

The HTML report should make these fields visible as separate blocks so an implementer can answer: what exact value does the design require, what exact value does the code implement, why is that a mismatch, exactly where is the code, and what should be changed.

Use stable IDs and Japanese text when the existing report is Japanese. Keep dimensions consistent with existing findings when continuing a report.

## Severity Guidance

- `high`: missing feature/route, wrong critical business rule, destructive or externally visible behavior, missing required import/export/batch/PDF flow.
- `med`: partial implementation, wrong filtering/sorting/session behavior, missing non-critical columns or validation, incompatible screen behavior.
- `low`: copy/display/default-state differences, ambiguous design wording, minor workflow variance.

## Report Generation

If the project already has a report generator, use it. Otherwise copy or adapt `scripts/build_report.py` from this skill into the report directory, then run it from the repository root or pass paths as needed.

The HTML report must include:

- summary counts: total findings, severities, audited functions, functions with drift
- scope and remaining work
- per-function sections
- each finding with severity, confidence, dimension, audit aspects including label/message/translation-key differences when applicable, design reference, implementation reference, design expectation, implementation actual behavior, `comparisonRows`, mismatch reason, impact, fix target, required change, and evidence
- a comparison table with columns: 比較項目 / 設計書の値・要求 / 実装の値・挙動 / 乖離理由
- links or file paths precise enough for a developer to inspect
- short cited design and implementation snippets when available
- phase/Ph2 data only as hidden or secondary metadata, never as a report column or primary summary
- a function-level implementation audit section showing verdict counts, no-entry candidates, candidate source references, and the search terms that produced them
- a requirement-level difference list derived only from confirmed/uncertain findings, showing the exact design requirement, implementation behavior, mismatch reason, implementation refs, and a link back to the finding. Do not include broad unverified source-signal queues in the main HTML report.
- a focused untraced-requirement section for small, high-risk classes such as display-time timers, automatic redirects, and timeout navigation. Keep it separate from confirmed difference and implementation-gap counts, and include search terms/candidate refs so the next audit step is concrete.

After editing findings, validate JSON and regenerate HTML:

```bash
python3 -m json.tool path/to/design_impl_drift_report/findings.json
python3 path/to/design_impl_drift_report/build_report.py
```

## Quality Bar

- Treat the HTML design spec as source of truth even when implementation looks intentional.
- Cite exact file paths and line numbers whenever available.
- Distinguish `不在` from “not found yet”; use `不在` only after searching likely implementation areas.
- Avoid broad claims such as “not implemented” without search evidence or contrasting nearby implemented features.
- Do not classify Phase 2 planned non-implementation as a defect. A missing route, CSV, batch, API, table, or screen is only a drift finding when the design expects it in the audited implementation phase.
- For label/message drift, prefer exact line evidence and a verified render path over fuzzy string similarity. Fuzzy or near-match scans are candidate generators; they are not findings until source and display usage are confirmed.
- When an external reviewer or another agent reports candidates, verify each candidate against the HTML design and implementation source before adding it. Keep rejected or ambiguous candidates out of confirmed counts, or mark them `UNCERTAIN` with the unresolved reason.
- For absent implementations, include the searched terms/areas, nearby implementation files with line numbers, and a concrete reason those nearby files do not satisfy the design. Do not leave `implRef` as only `不在` in the final report.
- Reject vague findings. A finding is not complete until it names the exact design value and exact implementation value that differ.
- Keep the report cumulative and resumable.

  → 33件の主張・重要度・根本原因キーを一覧化する
  • 各主張を ee の実行経路・代替実装と照合する
  • 対象外・重複・重要度を横断比較し、指定形式に絞る

sheet-6-R065	カード詳細(登録・編集・削除)	項目定義	実装違い	P3	IO	カードセット欄の初期表示は「カードセットを選択」という選択肢が選ばれた状態になる	初期表示の選択肢文言は「選択してください」。src/Eccube/Form/Type/Admin/CardDetailType.php:117 の placeholder が admin.common.select で、src/Eccube/Resource/locale/messages.ja.yaml:1727 の訳語が「選択してください」。	項目表 2-9 行の初期値セルが「カードセットを選択」と定めているのに対し、実装の初期選択肢文言は「選択してください」で異なる。カードセット自体は単一選択で実装済み。
sheet-6-R098	カード詳細(登録・編集・削除)	機能仕様	未実装	P3	ふるまい	詳細情報が商品と紐づいているカードの編集画面では、詳細情報を追加するボタンが表示されない	詳細情報を追加ボタンは商品との紐づきに関係なく常に表示される。src/Eccube/Resource/template/admin/Card/edit.twig:194 で取得した商品紐づきの有無（noDeletableCardDetailIds）は詳細情報ごとの削除可否にしか使われず、src/Eccube/Resource/template/admin/Card/edit.twig:199-201 の追加ボタンには条件が無い。	削除不可文言の表示（同ブロックの sheet-6-R025/R114）は実装されているが、詳細情報を追加ボタンを非表示にする処理は実装のどこにも無い。src/Eccube/Controller/Admin/Card/CardController.php:249 の紐づき判定結果も追加ボタンの制御には渡っていない。
sheet-6-R136	カード詳細(登録・編集・削除)	現行仕様	未実装	P2	ふるまい	カードを保存できたとき、支店側システムへ当該カードIDが連携される	カード保存後に支店側へ連携する処理は無い。src/Eccube/Controller/Admin/Card/CardController.php:271-291 は永続化と画面メッセージだけで、支店連携の呼び出しは存在しない。同じ支店連携は src/Eccube/Service/Csv/Importer/Event/ProductCardImportHandler.php:197-205 でも未移植のTODOとしてコメントのまま残っている。	支店連携を行う仕組み自体がリポジトリに移植されておらず、カード保存・削除のいずれからも呼ばれていない。
sheet-7-R035	カード登録CSVアップロード	現行仕様	未実装	P2	ふるまい	取込を確定したあと、対象になったカードが支店システムへ通知され、その通知に失敗しても取込自体は成功として扱われる。	取込の確定までは行うが、支店システムへ更新を知らせる処理が存在しない。	取込は全行を1つのまとまりとして確定するところまでは実装されている（src/Eccube/Service/Csv/CardCsv.php:466-530）が、確定後に支店システムへ更新を知らせる処理がどこにも無い（src/Eccube/Service/Csv/CardCsv.php:458-530、src/Eccube/Controller/Admin/Card/CardCsvController.php:141-153）。ee では支店への更新通知は他機能でも未移植のままで、コメントだけが残っている（src/Eccube/Service/Csv/Importer/Event/ProductCardImportHandler.php:205-206）。
sheet-7-R043	カード登録CSVアップロード	現行仕様	実装違い	P3	ふるまい	同じカード名(英語)の行が2行目以降に現れたときは、その行で得た値でカードタイプ・カラー・固有色・サブタイプ・特殊タイプの紐付けを上書きしない。	同じカード名(英語)の行が何行あっても毎行で紐付けを入れ替えるため、最後の行の内容が残る。	重複除去（src/Eccube/Service/Csv/CardCsv.php:952-961）と、値が空の列では紐付けを変えない扱い（src/Eccube/Service/Csv/CardCsv.php:482-500 の各条件）は設計どおり。一方、同じカード名(英語)が2行目以降に現れたときの扱いが無く、2行目でも同名のカードを取り直して（src/Eccube/Service/Csv/CardCsv.php:475-478）その行の値で紐付けを入れ替えるため（src/Eccube/Service/Csv/CardCsv.php:871-934 の各置換）、1行目で作った紐付けが後続行の内容で上書きされる。
sheet-8-R076	カード登録CSV	項目定義	実装違い	P2	ふるまい	CSVのカード詳細IDに対応するカード詳細がまだ登録されていない行では、その行のカード詳細情報が新規に登録される。	カード詳細IDに値がある行で該当のカード詳細が見つからない（または別カードのものである）場合、『登録されていません』のエラーを出して取込全体を中止する（src/Eccube/Service/Csv/CardCsv.php:740-746）。新規登録されるのはカード詳細ID列が空のときだけ（src/Eccube/Service/Csv/CardCsv.php:737、src/Eccube/Service/Csv/CardCsv.php:748-752）。	識別ID 35 の備考は『同じカード詳細IDのカードが未登録の場合、カードの詳細情報を新規登録する／登録済みの場合、更新する』と2分岐で書かれており、識別ID 2（カード名(英語)）で未登録なら新規登録する実装（src/Eccube/Service/Csv/CardCsv.php:474-477）と同じ形を求めている。実装は未登録側をエラーにしており、単体テスト tests/Eccube/Tests/Service/Csv/CardCsvTest.php:389-409 もエラーを期待値として固定している。
sheet-9-R032	カードセット管理(一覧)	項目定義	実装違い	P3	IO	一覧の各行では3点リーダーを押下したときに編集・削除の操作が現れる。	各行に編集アイコンと削除アイコンを常時並べて表示しており、3点リーダーの部品が無い。	レイアウト図では行末が3点リーダーで、押下して編集・削除が現れる。実装は3点リーダーを持たず、鉛筆(編集)と×(削除)のアイコンを常時2つ並べている（app/template/admin/Cardset/index.twig:166-181）。表示される操作は同じだが画面部品の構成が設計と異なる。
sheet-9-R047	カードセット管理(一覧)	現行仕様	実装違い	P3	ふるまい	ページ番号を指定せず一覧を開いたときは、前回の表示件数・並び順・ページ番号を引き継がず初期状態（10件・リリース日降順・1ページ目）で表示し、前の画面から戻る操作のときだけ前回の表示条件を引き継ぐ。	一覧を開くと常に前回の表示件数・並び順が使われ、初期状態に戻す経路が無い。	実装には再開の指定に当たる入力の判別が無く、一覧を開くたびにセッションの並び順・表示件数をそのまま使う（src/Eccube/Controller/Admin/Card/CardsetController.php:56-72,87-90）。メニューから開き直しても前回の条件が残り、破棄されない。ee の他の一覧（例 src/Eccube/Controller/Admin/Product/ProductController.php:208）には再開指定の判別があるが、カードセット一覧には無い。
sheet-9-R053	カードセット管理(一覧)	現行仕様	実装違い	P2	ふるまい	収録カードを持つカードセットの削除では、そのカードセットの情報も登録済みのシンボル画像も一切変わらず、エラーメッセージを出して元の画面へ戻る。	削除の試行前にシンボル画像ファイルを消しているため、削除がエラーで中止されてもシンボル画像だけが失われ、一覧で画像が表示されなくなる。	収録カードを持つカードセットは外部キー違反となり削除されずエラーが出る（src/Eccube/Controller/Admin/Card/CardsetController.php:255-265）。しかし削除を試みる前にシンボル画像ファイルを消しているため（src/Eccube/Controller/Admin/Card/CardsetController.php:246-253）、削除できなかった場合でもカードセットのシンボル画像だけが失われる。設計は「削除を行わず」であり、画像も残っていなければならない。
sheet-9-R054	カードセット管理(一覧)	現行仕様	実装違い	P3	ふるまい	削除できたときは、直前に表示していたページ番号の一覧へ戻る。	削除後は常に1ページ目の一覧へ戻る。	削除後はページ番号を持たない一覧へ戻しており（src/Eccube/Controller/Admin/Card/CardsetController.php:280）、保持している表示中ページ番号（src/Eccube/Controller/Admin/Card/CardsetController.php:109 でセッションに入れている値）は使われない。3ページ目で削除しても1ページ目が表示される。
sheet-9-R056	カードセット管理(一覧)	現行仕様	実装違い	P2	ふるまい	並び順の指定が選択肢と一致しないときは無視し、直前まで採用していた並び順のまま一覧を表示する。	並び順は選択肢との照合が無く、一致しない値もそのまま採用され保持される。	表示件数は不一致なら無視され直前の値が残る（src/Eccube/Controller/Admin/Card/CardsetController.php:91-102）が、並び順は照合が無く、選択肢に無い値でもそのまま採用・保持される（src/Eccube/Controller/Admin/Card/CardsetController.php:58-72）。「直前まで採用していた値のまま表示する」にならない。

    17	
    18	use Eccube\Entity\Master\MtbCardDetail;
    19	use Eccube\Repository\Master\MtbCardDetailRepository;
    20	use Eccube\Repository\Master\MtbCardImageRepository;
    21	use Eccube\Service\S3AccessService;
    22	use Symfony\Component\Filesystem\Filesystem;
    23	
    24	/**
    25	 * 管理画面：カードセット単位のカード画像 ZIP を組み立てる。
    26	 */
    27	class CardsetDownloadAction
    28	{
    29	    private const DOWNLOAD_DIR = 'var/tmp/download/card_image';
    30	
    31	    /** ZIP 内の最上位ディレクトリ */
    32	    private const ZIP_ROOT_DIR = 'download';
    33	
    34	    public function __construct(
    35	        private readonly MtbCardDetailRepository $cardDetailRepository,
    36	        private readonly MtbCardImageRepository $cardImageRepository,
    37	        private readonly S3AccessService $s3AccessService,
    38	        private readonly Filesystem $filesystem,
    39	        private readonly string $kernelProjectDir,
    40	    ) {
    41	    }
    42	
    43	    /**
    44	     * 指定カードセット（またはカードセット未設定のプロモ相当）に紐づく画像を ZIP にまとめ、ファイルパスを返す。
    45	     *
    46	     * 作業ディレクトリは `kernel.project_dir/var/tmp/download/card_image` に固定する。呼び出しのたびに当該ディレクトリを
    47	     * 削除してから再作成する（旧システムの仕様を踏襲）。
    48	     * 同一パスを複数リクエストが並行して使うと競合するため、当機能の同時実行は運用上避ける前提とする。
    49	     * 異常終了時は当該ディレクトリに残骸が残り得るが、次回成功パスの開始時に除去される。
    50	     *
    51	     * $ids が空のときはカードセット未設定のカード詳細のみを対象とする。
    52	     * カード画像は mtb_card_image.language_id 昇順で取得し、$eachLang が false のときは S3 取得に成功した先頭 1 枚のみ ZIP に含める。
    53	     * ZIP 内のパスは `download/{中間フォルダ}/{ファイル名}`。
    54	     * セット別は中間フォルダがセット code またはプロモの name_en（カードセット未設定時）。
    55	     * 言語別は中間フォルダは言語 code のみ（プロモ名・セット略称のフォルダは付けない）。ファイル名は `{F}{カード詳細ID}.jpg`（フォイル時のみ先頭に F）。
    56	     *
    57	     * @param array<int|string> $ids カードセット ID のリスト
    58	     * @param bool $eachLang true のとき言語コードごとのフォルダに全言語分を試行する
    59	     *
    60	     * @return string 生成された ZIP の絶対パス
    61	     *
    62	     * @throws \RuntimeException 追加画像が 0 件、ZIP の生成に失敗した場合、またはフォルダ名にパス区切り等が含まれる場合
    63	     */
    64	    public function execute(array $ids, bool $eachLang = false): string
    65	    {
    66	        set_time_limit(0);
    67	
    68	        $this->s3AccessService->setS3Directory('set/');
    69	
    70	        $workRoot = $this->kernelProjectDir.'/'.self::DOWNLOAD_DIR;
    71	        if ($this->filesystem->exists($workRoot)) {
    72	            $this->filesystem->remove($workRoot);
    73	        }
    74	        $this->filesystem->mkdir($workRoot, 0777);
    75	
    76	        $zipPath = $workRoot.'/card_image.zip';
    77	        $zip = new \ZipArchive();
    78	        if ($zip->open($zipPath, \ZipArchive::CREATE | \ZipArchive::OVERWRITE) !== true) {
    79	            throw new \RuntimeException('admin.card.cardset.error.download_zip_failed');
    80	        }
    81	
    82	        $addedCount = 0;
    83	
    84	        if ($ids === []) {
    85	            $idList = [null];
    86	        } else {
    87	            $idList = array_values(array_unique(array_filter(
    88	                array_map(
    89	                    static fn (int|string $id): int => filter_var($id, FILTER_VALIDATE_INT) !== false ? (int) $id : 0,
    90	                    $ids
    91	                ),
    92	                static fn (int $id): bool => $id > 0
    93	            )));
    94	        }
    95	
    96	        foreach ($idList as $cardsetId) {
    97	            $cardDetails = $this->cardDetailRepository->findWithImagesAndPromotionForDownload($cardsetId);
    98	            if ($cardDetails === []) {
    99	                continue;
   100	            }
   101	
   102	            $cardDetailIds = array_map(
   103	                static fn (MtbCardDetail $cd): int => $cd->getId(),
   104	                $cardDetails
   105	            );
   106	            $cardImagesByDetailId = [];
   107	            foreach ($this->cardImageRepository->findByCardDetailIdsForDownloadOrderedByLanguage($cardDetailIds) as $cardImage) {
   108	                $detail = $cardImage->getCardDetail();
   109	                if ($detail === null) {
   110	                    continue;
   111	                }
   112	                $cardImagesByDetailId[$detail->getId()][] = $cardImage;
   113	            }
   114	
   115	            foreach ($cardDetails as $cardDetail) {
   116	                $cardImages = $cardImagesByDetailId[$cardDetail->getId()] ?? [];
   117	
   118	                foreach ($cardImages as $cardImage) {
   119	                    $language = $cardImage->getLanguage();
   120	                    if ($language === null) {
   121	                        continue;
   122	                    }
   123	
   124	                    // 言語別: ZIP は download/{言語コード}/{F}{カード詳細ID}.jpg のみ（プロモ名フォルダは付けない）
   125	                    // セット別: download/{セット略称|プロモ英名}/{先頭1枚の画像ファイル名}
   126	                    if ($eachLang) {
   127	                        $zipMiddleDir = $this->requireSafePathSegment($language->getCode());
   128	                    } elseif ($cardDetail->getCardset() !== null) {
   129	                        $code = $cardDetail->getCardset()->getCode();
   130	                        $zipMiddleDir = $this->requireSafePathSegment($code ?? '');
   131	                    } else {
   132	                        $zipMiddleDir = $this->requireSafePathSegment($cardDetail->getPromotion()->getNameEn());
   133	                    }
   134	
   135	                    $dirName = $workRoot.'/'.$zipMiddleDir;
   136	                    if (!$this->filesystem->exists($dirName)) {
   137	                        $this->filesystem->mkdir($dirName, 0777);
   138	                    }
   139	
   140	                    $fileName = $this->s3AccessService->downloadCardImageToDirectory(
   141	                        $dirName,
   142	                        $cardImage,
   143	                        $cardDetail->getFoilFlg()
   144	                    );
   145	
   146	                    if ($fileName) {
   147	                        $fileName = $this->requireSafeZipEntryFileName($fileName);
   148	                    }
   149	
   150	                    if ($fileName && $this->filesystem->exists($dirName.'/'.$fileName)) {
   151	                        $localPath = $dirName.'/'.$fileName;
   152	                        $entryName = self::ZIP_ROOT_DIR.'/'.$zipMiddleDir.'/'.$fileName;
   153	                        if ($zip->addFile($localPath, $entryName)) {
   154	                            ++$addedCount;
   155	                        }
   156	
   157	                        // 言語別でない場合は1枚見つかったら次のカードへ（日本語優先）
   158	                        if (!$eachLang) {
   159	                            break;
   160	                        }
   161	                    }
   162	                }
   163	            }
   164	        }
   165	
   166	        $closed = $zip->close();
   167	
   168	        if ($addedCount === 0) {
   169	            if ($this->filesystem->exists($zipPath)) {
   170	                $this->filesystem->remove($zipPath);
   171	            }
   172	
   173	            throw new \RuntimeException('admin.card.cardset.error.download_no_images');
   174	        }
   175	
   176	        if (!$closed || !$this->filesystem->exists($zipPath)) {
   177	            throw new \RuntimeException('admin.card.cardset.error.download_zip_failed');
   178	        }
   179	
   180	        return $zipPath;
   181	    }
   182	
   183	    /**
   184	     * 作業ディレクトリ直下の1段のフォルダ名として安全な文字列か検証する。
   185	     * `/`・`\`・NUL、および trim 後が空・`.`・`..` の値は拒否する（パストラバーサル防止）。
   186	     *
   187	     * @throws \RuntimeException 上記に該当する場合（メッセージキー admin.card.cardset.error.download_unsafe_path_segment）
   188	     */
   189	    private function requireSafePathSegment(string $raw): string
   190	    {
   191	        if (str_contains($raw, "\0")) {
   192	            throw new \RuntimeException('admin.card.cardset.error.download_unsafe_path_segment');
   193	        }
   194	        $segment = trim($raw);
   195	        if ($segment === '' || $segment === '.' || $segment === '..') {
   196	            throw new \RuntimeException('admin.card.cardset.error.download_unsafe_path_segment');
   197	        }
   198	        if (strpbrk($segment, '/\\') !== false) {
   199	            throw new \RuntimeException('admin.card.cardset.error.download_unsafe_path_segment');
   200	        }
   201	
   202	        return $segment;
   203	    }
   204	
   205	    /**
   206	     * ZIP エントリ内のファイル名成分として安全か検証する（Zip slip 防止）。
   207	     *
   208	     * @throws \RuntimeException ディレクトリ成分を含む場合など
   209	     */
   210	    private function requireSafeZipEntryFileName(string $name): string
   211	    {
   212	        if (str_contains($name, "\0") || strpbrk($name, '/\\') !== false) {
   213	            throw new \RuntimeException('admin.card.cardset.error.download_unsafe_path_segment');
   214	        }
   215	        if ($name === '' || $name === '.' || $name === '..') {
   216	            throw new \RuntimeException('admin.card.cardset.error.download_unsafe_path_segment');
   217	        }
   218	        if (basename($name) !== $name) {
   219	            throw new \RuntimeException('admin.card.cardset.error.download_unsafe_path_segment');
   220	        }
   221	
   222	        return $name;
   223	    }
   224	}

    53	    #[Template('@admin/Cardset/index.twig')]
    54	    public function index(Request $request, \Knp\Component\Pager\PaginatorInterface $paginator, int $page_no = 1): array
    55	    {
    56	        // sort の処理
    57	        $sortKeySessionKey = 'eccube.admin.cardset.sort';
    58	        $sortKey = match (true) {
    59	            !empty($request->query->getString('sort_key')) => $request->query->getString('sort_key'),
    60	            ($this->session->get($sortKeySessionKey) !== null) => $this->session->get($sortKeySessionKey),
    61	            default => MtbCardsetRepository::DEFAULT_SORT_KEY,
    62	        };
    63	        $this->session->set($sortKeySessionKey, $sortKey);
    64	
    65	        // order の処理
    66	        $sortOrderSessionKey = 'eccube.admin.cardset.order';
    67	        $sortOrder = match (true) {
    68	            !empty($request->query->getString('sort_order')) => $request->query->getString('sort_order'),
    69	            ($this->session->get($sortOrderSessionKey) !== null) => $this->session->get($sortOrderSessionKey),
    70	            default => MtbCardsetRepository::DEFAULT_SORT_ORDER,
    71	        };
    72	        $this->session->set($sortOrderSessionKey, $sortOrder);
    73	
    74	        $qb = $this->cardsetRepository->getQueryBuilder([
    75	            'sortKey' => $sortKey,
    76	            'sortOrder' => $sortOrder,
    77	        ]);
    78	
    79	        $event = new EventArgs(
    80	            [
    81	                'qb' => $qb,
    82	            ],
    83	            $request
    84	        );
    85	        $this->eventDispatcher->dispatch($event, EccubeEvents::ADMIN_CARD_CARDSET_INDEX_INITIALIZE);
    86	
    87	        $pageCountSessionKey = 'eccube.admin.cardset.search.page_count';
    88	        $pageCount = $this->session->get($pageCountSessionKey,
    89	            $this->eccubeConfig->get('eccube_default_page_count'));
    90	
    91	        $pageCountParam = $request->query->getInt('page_count');
    92	        $PageMaxis = $this->pageMaxRepository->findAll();
    93	        if ($pageCountParam) {
    94	            foreach ($PageMaxis as $PageMax) {
    95	                $pageMaxNum = (int) $PageMax->getName();
    96	                if ($pageCountParam === $pageMaxNum) {
    97	                    $pageCount = $pageMaxNum;
    98	                    $this->session->set($pageCountSessionKey, $pageCount);
    99	                    break;
   100	                }
   101	            }
   102	        }
   103	        $pagination = $paginator->paginate(
   104	            $qb,
   105	            $page_no,
   106	            $pageCount
   107	        );
   108	
   109	        $this->session->set('eccube.admin.cardset.page_no', $page_no);
   110	
   111	        return [
   112	            'pagination' => $pagination,
   113	            'pageMaxis' => $PageMaxis,
   114	            'pageCount' => $pageCount,
   115	            'sortKeys' => $this->cardsetRepository->getSortKeys(),
   116	            'sortKey' => $sortKey,
   117	            'sortOrder' => $sortOrder,
   118	        ];
   119	    }

   127	    #[Template('@admin/Cardset/edit.twig')]
   128	    public function new(Request $request, CardsetSaveAction $saveAction): RedirectResponse|array
   129	    {
   130	        $Cardset = new MtbCardset();
   131	        $builder = $this->formFactory->createBuilder(CardsetType::class, $Cardset, ['cardset_id' => null]);
   132	
   133	        $event = new EventArgs(
   134	            [
   135	                'builder' => $builder,
   136	                'Cardset' => $Cardset,
   137	            ],
   138	            $request
   139	        );
   140	        $this->eventDispatcher->dispatch($event, EccubeEvents::ADMIN_CARD_CARDSET_NEW_INITIALIZE);
   141	
   142	        $form = $builder->getForm();
   143	        $form->handleRequest($request);
   144	
   145	        if ($form->isSubmitted() && $form->isValid()) {
   146	            $Cardset = $form->getData();
   147	            $saveAction->handle($Cardset);
   148	
   149	            $event = new EventArgs(
   150	                [
   151	                    'form' => $form,
   152	                    'Cardset' => $Cardset,
   153	                ],
   154	                $request
   155	            );
   156	            $this->eventDispatcher->dispatch($event, EccubeEvents::ADMIN_CARD_CARDSET_NEW_COMPLETE);
   157	
   158	            $this->addSuccess('admin.common.save_complete', 'admin');
   159	
   160	            return $this->redirectToRoute('admin_cardset_edit', ['id' => $Cardset->getId()]);
   161	        }
   162	
   163	        $pageNo = $this->session->get('eccube.admin.cardset.page_no', 1);
   164	
   165	        return [
   166	            'form' => $form->createView(),
   167	            'Cardset' => $Cardset,
   168	            'pageNo' => $pageNo,
   169	        ];
   170	    }

   177	    #[Route('/%eccube_admin_route%/cardset/{id}/edit', name: 'admin_cardset_edit', methods: ['GET', 'POST'])]
   178	    #[Template('@admin/Cardset/edit.twig')]
   179	    public function edit(Request $request, int $id, CardsetSaveAction $saveAction): RedirectResponse|array
   180	    {
   181	        if (!$Cardset = $this->cardsetRepository->find($id)) {
   182	            throw new NotFoundHttpException();
   183	        }
   184	        $builder = $this->formFactory->createBuilder(CardsetType::class, $Cardset, ['cardset_id' => $id]);
   185	
   186	        $event = new EventArgs(
   187	            [
   188	                'builder' => $builder,
   189	                'Cardset' => $Cardset,
   190	            ],
   191	            $request
   192	        );
   193	        $this->eventDispatcher->dispatch($event, EccubeEvents::ADMIN_CARD_CARDSET_EDIT_INITIALIZE);
   194	
   195	        $form = $builder->getForm();
   196	        $form->handleRequest($request);
   197	
   198	        if ($form->isSubmitted() && $form->isValid()) {
   199	            $Cardset = $form->getData();
   200	            $saveAction->handle($Cardset);
   201	
   202	            $event = new EventArgs(
   203	                [
   204	                    'form' => $form,
   205	                    'Cardset' => $Cardset,
   206	                ],
   207	                $request
   208	            );
   209	            $this->eventDispatcher->dispatch($event, EccubeEvents::ADMIN_CARD_CARDSET_EDIT_COMPLETE);
   210	
   211	            $this->addSuccess('admin.common.save_complete', 'admin');
   212	
   213	            return $this->redirectToRoute('admin_cardset_edit', ['id' => $Cardset->getId()]);
   214	        }
   215	
   216	        // 収録カードリスト
   217	        $cardList = $this->cardRepository->getQueryBuilderByCardset($Cardset)
   218	            ->getQuery()
   219	            ->getResult();
   220	
   221	        $pageNo = $this->session->get('eccube.admin.cardset.page_no', 1);
   222	
   223	        return [
   224	            'form' => $form->createView(),
   225	            'Cardset' => $Cardset,
   226	            'cardList' => $cardList,
   227	            'pageNo' => $pageNo,
   228	        ];
   229	    }

   234	    #[Route(
   235	        '/%eccube_admin_route%/product/cardset/{id}/delete',
   236	        requirements: ['id' => '\d+'],

   238	        methods: ['DELETE']

   240	    public function delete(Request $request, MtbCardset $Cardset): RedirectResponse
   241	    {
   242	        $this->isTokenValid();
   243	
   244	        log_info('カードセット削除開始', [$Cardset->getId()]);
   245	
   246	        if ($symbolImage = $Cardset->getSymbolImage()) {
   247	            // シンボル画像ファイルを削除
   248	            $fileAdapter = $this->fileManager->getAdapter();
   249	
   250	            if ($fileAdapter->exists($symbolImage, [])) {
   251	                $fileAdapter->delete($symbolImage, []);
   252	            }
   253	        }
   254	
   255	        try {
   256	            $this->cardsetRepository->delete($Cardset);
   257	            $this->entityManager->flush();
   258	
   259	            $this->addSuccess('admin.common.delete_complete', 'admin');
   260	            log_info('カードセット削除完了', [$Cardset->getId()]);
   261	        } catch (\Doctrine\DBAL\Exception\ForeignKeyConstraintViolationException $e) {
   262	            log_error('カードセット削除エラー（外部キー制約）', [$Cardset->getId(), $e]);
   263	
   264	            $message = trans('admin.card.cardset.error.delete_has_cards', ['%name%' => $Cardset->getNameJp()]);
   265	            $this->addError($message, 'admin');
   266	        } catch (\Exception $e) {
   267	            log_error('カードセット削除エラー', [$Cardset->getId(), $e]);
   268	
   269	            $this->addError(trans('admin.common.delete_error'), 'admin');
   270	        }
   271	
   272	        $event = new EventArgs(
   273	            [
   274	                'Cardset' => $Cardset,
   275	            ],
   276	            $request
   277	        );
   278	        $this->eventDispatcher->dispatch($event, EccubeEvents::ADMIN_CARD_CARDSET_DELETE_COMPLETE);
   279	
   280	        return $this->redirectToRoute('admin_cardset_list');
   281	    }

   290	    {
   291	        $ids = $this->getCardsetIdsFromRequest($request);
   292

   294	            $zipPath = $downloadAction->execute($ids, false);
   295	
   296	            $event = new EventArgs([], $request);

   298	
   299	            return $this->createZipResponse($zipPath);

   301	            $this->addError(trans($e->getMessage()), 'admin');
   302	
   303	            return $this->redirectToRoute('admin_cardset_list');
   304	        }
   305	    }

     3	declare(strict_types=1);
     4

    15	
    16	namespace Eccube\Service;
    17	
    18	use Aws\Result;
    19	use Aws\S3\Exception\S3Exception;
    20	use Aws\S3\S3Client;
    21	use Eccube\Entity\Master\MtbCardImage;
    22	use Eccube\Service\S3\S3BucketConfig;
    23	
    24	class S3AccessService
    25	{
    26	    private S3Client $client;
    27	    private string $s3Directory = '';
    28	    private string $bucket;
    29	
    30	    public function __construct(
    31	        S3BucketConfig $config,
    32	    ) {
    33	        $this->client = new S3Client([
    34	            'region' => $config->region,
    35	            'version' => $config->version,
    36	            'credentials' => $config->credentials,
    37	            'endpoint' => $config->endpoint,
    38	            'use_path_style_endpoint' => $config->usePathStyleEndpoint,
    39	        ]);
    40	
    41	        $this->bucket = $config->bucket;
    42	    }
    43	
    44	    /**
    45	     * upload script
    46	     *
    47	     * @param array{extension: string, file: string|object} $fileInfo
    48	     */
    49	    public function putFile(array $fileInfo, string $suffix = ''): string
    50	    {
    51	        do {
    52	            $fileName = $this->s3Directory.uniqid().rand(100, 999).$suffix.'.'.strtolower($fileInfo['extension']);
    53	        } while ($this->client->doesObjectExist($this->bucket, $fileName));
    54	
    55	        $this->client->putObject([
    56	            'Bucket' => $this->bucket,
    57	            'Key' => $fileName,
    58	            'SourceFile' => $fileInfo['file'], // EntityBody::factoryの代わり
    59	            'ContentType' => is_object($fileInfo['file']) && method_exists($fileInfo['file'], 'getMimeType')
    60	                ? $fileInfo['file']->getMimeType()
    61	                : null,
    62	        ]);
    63	
    64	        return $fileName;
    65	    }
    66	
    67	    /**
    68	     * ファイルパスでなくファイルの中身を直接アップロードする
    69	     *
    70	     * @param array{extension: string, file: string|object} $fileInfo
    71	     */
    72	    public function putFileData(array $fileInfo, string $suffix = ''): string
    73	    {
    74	        do {
    75	            $fileName = $this->s3Directory.uniqid().rand(100, 999).$suffix.'.'.strtolower((string) $fileInfo['extension']);
    76	        } while ($this->client->doesObjectExist($this->bucket, $fileName));
    77	
    78	        $this->client->putObject([
    79	            'Bucket' => $this->bucket,
    80	            'Key' => $fileName,
    81	            'Body' => $fileInfo['file'],
    82	            'ContentType' => is_object($fileInfo['file']) && method_exists($fileInfo['file'], 'getMimeType')
    83	                ? $fileInfo['file']->getMimeType()
    84	                : null,
    85	        ]);
    86	
    87	        return $fileName;
    88	    }
    89	
    90	    public function setS3Directory(string $dir): self
    91	    {
    92	        $this->s3Directory = $dir;
    93	
    94	        return $this;
    95	    }
    96	
    97	    /**
    98	     * @return array<string, mixed>
    99	     */
   100	    public function getFiles(string $dir, ?string $key = null): array
   101	    {
   102	        $objects = $this->client->listObjects([
   103	            'Bucket' => $this->bucket,
   104	            'Prefix' => $dir,
   105	            'Marker' => $key,
   106	        ]);
   107	
   108	        $contents = [];
   109	        if (isset($objects['Contents'])) {
   110	            foreach ($objects['Contents'] as $object) {
   111	                if (!str_ends_with($object['Key'], '/')) {
   112	                    $contents[] = $object;
   113	                }
   114	            }
   115	        }
   116	
   117	        if ($objects['IsTruncated']) {
   118	            $lastKey = end($contents)['Key'];
   119	            $contents = array_merge($contents, $this->getFiles($dir, $lastKey));
   120	        }
   121	
   122	        return $contents;
   123	    }
   124	
   125	    public function isFileExist(string $fileName): bool
   126	    {
   127	        return $this->client->doesObjectExist($this->bucket, $fileName);
   128	    }
   129	
   130	    /**
   131	     * カード画像をローカルディレクトリへ保存し、保存したファイル名を返す（旧 HareruyaEc S3AccessService::downloadFile 相当）.
   132	     */
   133	    public function downloadCardImageToDirectory(string $dirName, MtbCardImage $cardImage, int $foilFlg = 0): ?string
   134	    {
   135	        $ext = '.jpg';
   136	        $s3Key = ltrim(trim((string) $cardImage->getUrl()), '/');
   137	        $prefix = trim($this->s3Directory, '/');
   138	        if ($prefix !== '' && !str_starts_with($s3Key, $prefix.'/')) {
   139	            $s3Key = $prefix.'/'.$s3Key;
   140	        }
   141	
   142	        $cardDetail = $cardImage->getCardDetail();
   143	        if ($cardDetail === null || $cardDetail->getId() === null) {
   144	            return null;
   145	        }
   146	
   147	        $localFileName = $cardDetail->getId().$ext;
   148	        if ($foilFlg !== 0) {
   149	            $localFileName = 'F'.$localFileName;
   150	        }
   151	
   152	        try {
   153	            $this->client->getObject([
   154	                'Bucket' => $this->bucket,
   155	                'Key' => $s3Key,
   156	                '@http' => ['sink' => $dirName.'/'.$localFileName],
   157	            ]);
   158	        } catch (S3Exception $e) {
   159	            log_info('カード画像S3取得エラー', [$e->getMessage()]);
   160	
   161	            return null;
   162	        }
   163	
   164	        return $localFileName;
   165	    }
   166	
   167	    /**
   168	     * @return Result<mixed>
   169	     */
   170	    public function deleteFile(string $key): Result
   171	    {
   172	        return $this->client->deleteObject([
   173	            'Bucket' => $this->bucket,
   174	            'Key' => $key,
   175	        ]);
   176	    }
   177	
   178	    public function getBase64(string $fileName): string
   179	    {
   180	        try {
   181	            $result = $this->client->getObject([
   182	                'Bucket' => $this->bucket,
   183	                'Key' => $fileName,
   184	            ]);
   185	
   186	            return base64_encode((string) $result['Body']);
   187	        } catch (S3Exception $e) {
   188	            return '';
   189	        }
   190	    }
   191	
   192	    /**
   193	     * @return Result<mixed>|string
   194	     */
   195	    public function getFile(string $fileName): Result|string
   196	    {
   197	        try {
   198	            // ファイル情報取得
   199	            $result = $this->client->getObject([
   200	                'Bucket' => $this->bucket,
   201	                'Key' => $fileName,
   202	            ]);
   203	        } catch (S3Exception $e) {
   204	            log_info('身分証データ取得エラー', [$e->getMessage()]);
   205	
   206	            return '';
   207	        }
   208	
   209	        return $result;
   210	    }

    58	    /**
    59	     * 支店表示フラグが立った発売済みのカードセットを取得
    60	     */
    61	    public function getBranchDisplayCardsets(): mixed
    62	    {
    63	        $cardSets = $this->createQueryBuilder('cs')
    64	        ->where('cs.branch_display_flg = true')
    65	        ->orderBy('cs.release_date', 'DESC')
    66	        ->getQuery()
    67	        ->getResult();
    68

    80	
    81	    /**
    82	     * 全カードセットの階層 path を `cardsetId => [親ラベル, ...] (root→leaf)` で返す。
    83	     * dtb_category がセットされていればその階層、なければ mtb_cardsetblock 名を 1 階層親とする。
    84	     * サジェスト表示時に cardset_id から階層 label をフロントで lookup するために使う。
    85	     *
    86	     * @return array<int, list<string>>
    87	     */
    88	    public function getCardsetCategoryPathMap(string $locale = 'ja'): array
    89	    {
    90	        $em = $this->getEntityManager();
    91	        $categoryRows = $em->createQueryBuilder()
    92	            ->select('c.id', 'c.name', 'IDENTITY(c.Parent) AS parent_id')
    93	            ->from(\Eccube\Entity\Category::class, 'c')
    94	            ->getQuery()
    95	            ->getArrayResult();
    96	        $categoryNameById = [];
    97	        $categoryParentById = [];
    98	        foreach ($categoryRows as $row) {
    99	            $id = (int) $row['id'];
   100	            $categoryNameById[$id] = (string) $row['name'];
   101	            $categoryParentById[$id] = $row['parent_id'] !== null ? (int) $row['parent_id'] : null;
   102	        }
   103	
   104	        $cardsetRows = $this->createQueryBuilder('cs')
   105	            ->select('cs.id', 'IDENTITY(cs.Category) AS category_id', 'IDENTITY(cs.Cardsetblock) AS block_id')
   106	            ->getQuery()
   107	            ->getArrayResult();
   108	        $blockRows = $em->createQueryBuilder()
   109	            ->select('b.id', 'b.name_jp', 'b.name_en')
   110	            ->from(\Eccube\Entity\Master\MtbCardsetblock::class, 'b')
   111	            ->getQuery()
   112	            ->getArrayResult();
   113	        $blockLabelById = [];
   114	        foreach ($blockRows as $row) {
   115	            $blockLabelById[(int) $row['id']] = $locale === 'en' ? (string) $row['name_en'] : (string) $row['name_jp'];
   116	        }
   117	
   118	        $map = [];
   119	        foreach ($cardsetRows as $row) {
   120	            $cardsetId = (int) $row['id'];
   121	            $names = [];
   122	            $categoryId = $row['category_id'] !== null ? (int) $row['category_id'] : null;
   123	            if ($categoryId !== null) {
   124	                $chain = [];
   125	                $current = $categoryId;
   126	                $guard = 10;
   127	                while ($current !== null && $guard-- > 0) {
   128	                    if (!isset($categoryNameById[$current])) {
   129	                        break;
   130	                    }
   131	                    $chain[] = $categoryNameById[$current];
   132	                    $current = $categoryParentById[$current] ?? null;
   133	                }
   134	                $names = array_reverse($chain);
   135	            }
   136	            $blockId = $row['block_id'] !== null ? (int) $row['block_id'] : null;
   137	            if ($blockId !== null && isset($blockLabelById[$blockId])) {
   138	                $names[] = $blockLabelById[$blockId];
   139	            }
   140	            $map[$cardsetId] = $names;
   141	        }
   142	
   143	        return $map;
   144	    }
   145	
   146	    /**
   147	     * リリース済みで最新のカードセットを取得
   148	     */
   149	    public function getRecentCardset(): ?MtbCardset
   150	    {
   151	        return $this->createQueryBuilder('cs')
   152	            ->where('cs.release_date <= :now')
   153	            ->setParameter('now', new \DateTime())
   154	            ->orderBy('cs.release_date', 'DESC')
   155	            ->setMaxResults(1)
   156	            ->getQuery()
   157	            ->getOneOrNullResult();
   158	    }
   159	
   160	    /**
   161	     * @param array<string, string> $sortData
   162	     */
   163	    public function getQueryBuilder(array $sortData = []): QueryBuilder
   164	    {
   165	        $qb = $this->createQueryBuilder('cs')
   166	            ->select('cs')
   167	        ;
   168	
   169	        // ソート
   170	        $sortKey = $sortData['sortKey'] ?? self::DEFAULT_SORT_KEY;
   171	        $order = $sortData['sortOrder'] ?? self::DEFAULT_SORT_ORDER;
   172	        $sortColumn = $this->sortKeys[$sortKey] ?? $this->sortKeys[self::DEFAULT_SORT_KEY];
   173	        $qb->addOrderBy($sortColumn, $order);
   174	
   175	        return $qb;
   176	    }

    60	    #[Template('@admin/Format/edit.twig')]
    61	    public function new(Request $request, FormatSaveAction $saveAction): RedirectResponse|array
    62	    {
    63	        $Format = new MtbFormat();
    64	        $builder = $this->formFactory->createBuilder(FormatType::class, $Format, ['format_id' => null]);
    65	
    66	        $event = new EventArgs(
    67	            [
    68	                'builder' => $builder,
    69	                'Format' => $Format,
    70	            ],
    71	            $request
    72	        );
    73	        $this->eventDispatcher->dispatch($event, EccubeEvents::ADMIN_CARD_FORMAT_NEW_INITIALIZE);
    74	
    75	        $form = $builder->getForm();
    76	        $form->handleRequest($request);
    77	
    78	        if ($form->isSubmitted() && $form->isValid()) {
    79	            $Format = $form->getData();
    80	            $saveAction->handle(new FormatSaveInput(
    81	                $Format,
    82	                $form['commandMaxCardCount']->getData(),
    83	                $form['commandMinCardCount']->getData(),
    84	                $form['commanderInMain']->getData(),
    85	            ));
    86	
    87	            $event = new EventArgs(
    88	                [
    89	                    'form' => $form,
    90	                    'Format' => $Format,
    91	                ],
    92	                $request
    93	            );
    94	            $this->eventDispatcher->dispatch($event, EccubeEvents::ADMIN_CARD_FORMAT_NEW_COMPLETE);
    95	
    96	            $this->addSuccess('admin.common.save_complete', 'admin');
    97	
    98	            return $this->redirectToRoute('admin_format_edit', ['id' => $Format->getId()]);
    99	        }
   100	
   101	        return [
   102	            'form' => $form->createView(),
   103	            'Format' => $Format,
   104	        ];
   105	    }

   112	    #[Route('/%eccube_admin_route%/format/{id}/edit', name: 'admin_format_edit', methods: ['GET', 'POST'])]
   113	    #[Template('@admin/Format/edit.twig')]
   114	    public function edit(Request $request, int $id, FormatSaveAction $saveAction): RedirectResponse|array
   115	    {
   116	        /** @var MtbFormat|null $Format */
   117	        $Format = $this->formatRepository->find($id);
   118	        if (is_null($Format)) {
   119	            throw new NotFoundHttpException();
   120	        }
   121	
   122	        $builder = $this->formFactory->createBuilder(FormatType::class, $Format, ['format_id' => $id]);
   123	
   124	        $event = new EventArgs(
   125	            [
   126	                'builder' => $builder,
   127	                'Format' => $Format,
   128	            ],
   129	            $request
   130	        );
   131	        $this->eventDispatcher->dispatch($event, EccubeEvents::ADMIN_CARD_FORMAT_EDIT_INITIALIZE);
   132	
   133	        $form = $builder->getForm();
   134	        $form->handleRequest($request);
   135	
   136	        if ($form->isSubmitted() && $form->isValid()) {
   137	            $Format = $form->getData();
   138	            $saveAction->handle(new FormatSaveInput(
   139	                $Format,
   140	                $form['commandMaxCardCount']->getData(),
   141	                $form['commandMinCardCount']->getData(),
   142	                $form['commanderInMain']->getData(),
   143	            ));
   144	
   145	            $event = new EventArgs(
   146	                [
   147	                    'form' => $form,
   148	                    'Format' => $Format,
   149	                ],
   150	                $request
   151	            );
   152	            $this->eventDispatcher->dispatch($event, EccubeEvents::ADMIN_CARD_FORMAT_EDIT_COMPLETE);
   153	
   154	            $this->addSuccess('admin.common.save_complete', 'admin');
   155	
   156	            return $this->redirectToRoute('admin_format_edit', ['id' => $Format->getId()]);
   157	        }
   158	
   159	        // 統率ボード上下限
   160	        if ($CommanderFormatBoard = $Format->getFormatBoardByBoardId(MtbBoard::BOARD_ID_COMMAND)) {
   161	            $form['commanderInMain']->setData($Format->isCommanderInMain());
   162	            $form['commandMaxCardCount']->setData($CommanderFormatBoard->getMaxCardCount());
   163	            $form['commandMinCardCount']->setData($CommanderFormatBoard->getMinCardCount());
   164	        }
   165	
   166	        return [
   167	            'form' => $form->createView(),
   168	            'Format' => $Format,
   169	        ];
   170	    }
   171	
   172	    #[Route('/%eccube_admin_route%/product/format/{id}/delete', name: 'admin_format_delete', requirements: ['id' => '\d+'], methods: ['DELETE'])]
   173	    public function delete(Request $request, MtbFormat $Format): RedirectResponse
   174	    {
   175	        $this->isTokenValid();
   176	
   177	        $formatId = $Format->getId();
   178	        $formatName = $Format->getNameJp();
   179	
   180	        log_info('フォーマット削除開始', [$formatId]);
   181	
   182	        try {
   183	            // 大会・デッキ・アーキタイプで使用されているなら削除不可とする
   184	            if (!$Format->getEvents()->isEmpty()) {
   185	                $this->addError('admin.product.format.error.delete.registered_event', 'admin');
   186	
   187	                return $this->redirectToRoute('admin_format_list');
   188	            }
   189	            if (!$Format->getDecks()->isEmpty()) {
   190	                $this->addError('admin.product.format.error.delete.registered_deck', 'admin');
   191	
   192	                return $this->redirectToRoute('admin_format_list');
   193	            }
   194	            if (!$Format->getArchetypes()->isEmpty()) {
   195	                $this->addError('admin.product.format.error.delete.registered_archetype', 'admin');
   196	
   197	                return $this->redirectToRoute('admin_format_list');
   198	            }
   199	
   200	            $this->entityManager->remove($Format);
   201	            $this->entityManager->flush();
   202	
   203	            $this->addSuccess('admin.common.delete_complete', 'admin');
   204	
   205	            log_info('フォーマット削除完了', [$formatId]);
   206	        } catch (\Exception $e) {
   207	            log_info('フォーマット削除エラー', [$formatId, $e]);
   208	
   209	            $message = trans('admin.common.delete_error_foreign_key', ['%name%' => $Format->getNameJp()]);
   210	            $this->addError($message, 'admin');
   211	        }
   212	
   213	        $event = new EventArgs(
   214	            [
   215	                'Format' => $Format,
   216	            ],
   217	            $request
   218	        );
   219	        $this->eventDispatcher->dispatch($event, EccubeEvents::ADMIN_CARD_FORMAT_DELETE_COMPLETE);
   220	
   221	        return $this->redirectToRoute('admin_format_list');
   222	    }
   223	}
   250	    }

     1	<?php
     2	
     3	declare(strict_types=1);
     4	
     5	/*
     6	 * This file is part of EC-CUBE
     7	 *
     8	 * Copyright(c) EC-CUBE CO.,LTD. All Rights Reserved.
     9	 *
    10	 * http://www.ec-cube.co.jp/
    11	 *
    12	 * For the full copyright and license information, please view the LICENSE
    13	 * file that was distributed with this source code.
    14	 */
    15	
    16	namespace Eccube\Service\Admin\Format;
    17	
    18	use Doctrine\ORM\EntityManagerInterface;
    19	use Eccube\Entity\Master\MtbBoard;
    20	use Eccube\Repository\Master\MtbBoardRepository;
    21	use Eccube\Service\Admin\Format\ActionInput\FormatSaveInput;
    22	use Eccube\Service\EntityManager\FormatBoardEntityManager;
    23	
    24	class FormatSaveAction
    25	{
    26	    private EntityManagerInterface $entityManager;
    27	    private FormatBoardEntityManager $formatBoardEntityManager;
    28	    private MtbBoardRepository $mtbBoardRepository;
    29	
    30	    public function __construct(
    31	        EntityManagerInterface $entityManager,
    32	        FormatBoardEntityManager $formatBoardEntityManager,
    33	        MtbBoardRepository $mtbBoardRepository,
    34	    ) {
    35	        $this->entityManager = $entityManager;
    36	        $this->formatBoardEntityManager = $formatBoardEntityManager;
    37	        $this->mtbBoardRepository = $mtbBoardRepository;
    38	    }
    39	
    40	    public function handle(FormatSaveInput $input): void
    41	    {
    42	        $this->entityManager->beginTransaction();
    43	
    44	        try {
    45	            $this->entityManager->persist($input->Format);
    46	
    47	            $this->persistEachBoards($input);
    48	
    49	            $this->entityManager->flush();
    50	            $this->entityManager->commit();
    51	        } catch (\Exception $e) {
    52	            $this->entityManager->rollback();
    53	
    54	            throw $e;
    55	        }
    56	    }
    57	
    58	    /**
    59	     * 各ボード情報をpersistする
    60	     */
    61	    protected function persistEachBoards(FormatSaveInput $input): void
    62	    {
    63	        $Format = $input->Format;
    64	
    65	        // メイン
    66	        $MainBoard = $this->mtbBoardRepository->find(MtbBoard::BOARD_ID_MAIN);
    67	        $MainFormatBoard = $this->formatBoardEntityManager->save(
    68	            $Format,
    69	            $MainBoard,
    70	            $Format->getMainMaxCardCount(),
    71	            $Format->getMainMinCardCount(),
    72	            null
    73	        );
    74	
    75	        // サイド
    76	        $SideBoard = $this->mtbBoardRepository->find(MtbBoard::BOARD_ID_SIDE);
    77	        $this->formatBoardEntityManager->save(
    78	            $Format,
    79	            $SideBoard,
    80	            $Format->getSideMaxCardCount(),
    81	            $Format->getSideMinCardCount(),
    82	            null
    83	        );
    84	
    85	        // 統率
    86	        if ($Format->isUseCommandFlg()) {
    87	            $CommandBoard = $this->mtbBoardRepository->find(MtbBoard::BOARD_ID_COMMAND);
    88	            $this->formatBoardEntityManager->save(
    89	                $Format,
    90	                $CommandBoard,
    91	                $input->commandMaxCardCount,
    92	                $input->commandMinCardCount,
    93	                $input->commanderInMain ? $MainFormatBoard : null
    94	            );
    95	        } elseif ($CommandFormatBoard = $Format->getFormatBoardByBoardId(MtbBoard::BOARD_ID_COMMAND)) {
    96	            $this->entityManager->remove($CommandFormatBoard);
    97	        }
    98	    }
    99	}

    16	namespace Eccube\Tests\Form\Type\Admin;
    17	
    18	use Eccube\Entity\Master\MtbCertification;
    19	use Eccube\Entity\Master\MtbFormat;
    20	use Eccube\Form\Type\Admin\FormatType;
    21	use Symfony\Component\Form\FormInterface;
    22	
    23	class FormatTypeTest extends \Eccube\Tests\Form\Type\AbstractTypeTestCase
    24	{
    25	    /**
    26	     * @var FormInterface
    27	     */
    28	    protected FormInterface $form;
    29	
    30	    /**
    31	     * {@inheritdoc}
    32	     */
    33	    protected function setUp(): void
    34	    {
    35	        parent::setUp();
    36	
    37	        // CSRF 無効でフォーム作成
    38	        $this->form = $this->formFactory
    39	            ->createBuilder(FormatType::class, new MtbFormat(), [
    40	                'csrf_protection' => false,
    41	                'format_id' => null,
    42	            ])
    43	            ->getForm();
    44	    }
    45	
    46	    /**
    47	     * nameJp / nameEn / rank - 異常系
    48	     * 必須項目が未入力の場合はエラーになること
    49	     *
    50	     * @group HARERUYA
    51	     */
    52	    public function testRequiredFieldsInvalid(): void
    53	    {
    54	        $this->form->submit([]);
    55	
    56	        $this->assertFalse($this->form->isValid());
    57	        $this->assertTrue($this->form['nameJp']->getErrors()->count() > 0);
    58	        $this->assertTrue($this->form['nameEn']->getErrors()->count() > 0);
    59	        $this->assertTrue($this->form['rank']->getErrors()->count() > 0);
    60	    }
    61	
    62	    /**
    63	     * rank - 異常系
    64	     * 数値以外が入力された場合はバリデーションエラーとなること
    65	     *
    66	     * @group HARERUYA
    67	     */
    68	    public function testRankInvalidType(): void
    69	    {
    70	        $inputValues = [
    71	            'nameJp' => 'テスト',
    72	            'nameEn' => 'Test',
    73	            'rank' => 'abc',
    74	        ];
    75	
    76	        $this->form->submit($inputValues);
    77	
    78	        $this->assertFalse($this->form->isValid());
    79	        $this->assertTrue($this->form['rank']->getErrors()->count() > 0);
    80	    }
    81	
    82	    /**
    83	     * mainMaxCardCount - 異常系
    84	     * 上限超過の場合はバリデーションエラーとなること
    85	     *
    86	     * @group HARERUYA
    87	     */
    88	    public function testMainMaxCardCountOutOfRange(): void
    89	    {
    90	        $inputValues = [
    91	            'nameJp' => 'テスト',
    92	            'nameEn' => 'Test',
    93	            'rank' => 1,
    94	            'mainMaxCardCount' => $this->eccubeConfig['eccube_construct_format_board_max_card_count'] + 1,
    95	        ];
    96	
    97	        $this->form->submit($inputValues);
    98	
    99	        $this->assertFalse($this->form->isValid());
   100	        $this->assertTrue($this->form['mainMaxCardCount']->getErrors()->count() > 0);
   101	    }
   102	
   103	    /**
   104	     * MetaRange - 正常系
   105	     * 範囲内の値が入力された場合はバリデーションが成功すること
   106	     *
   107	     * @group HARERUYA
   108	     */
   109	    public function testMetaRangeValid(): void
   110	    {
   111	        $inputValues = [
   112	            'nameJp' => 'テスト',
   113	            'nameEn' => 'Test',
   114	            'rank' => 1,
   115	            'MetaRange' => $this->eccubeConfig['eccube_construct_format_meta_range_min_days'],
   116	        ];
   117	
   118	        $this->form->submit($inputValues);
   119	
   120	        $this->assertTrue($this->form->isValid());
   121	    }
   122	
   123	    /**
   124	     * Certification - 正常系
   125	     * 有効な認定が選択された場合はバリデーションが成功すること
   126	     *
   127	     * @group HARERUYA
   128	     */
   129	    public function testCertificationValid(): void
   130	    {
   131	        // 先頭の認定マスタを取得
   132	        $Certification = $this->entityManager
   133	            ->getRepository(MtbCertification::class)
   134	            ->findOneBy([]);
   135	
   136	        if ($Certification === null) {
   137	            $this->markTestSkipped('MtbCertification が存在しないためスキップ');
   138	        }
   139	
   140	        $inputValues = [
   141	            'nameJp' => 'テスト',
   142	            'nameEn' => 'Test',
   143	            'rank' => 1,
   144	            'Certification' => $Certification->getId(),
   145	        ];
   146	
   147	        $this->form->submit($inputValues);
   148	
   149	        $this->assertTrue($this->form->isValid());
   150	    }
   151	
   152	    /**
   153	     * mapped=false フィールド - 正常系
   154	     * submit しても Entity に影響しないこと
   155	     *
   156	     * @group HARERUYA
   157	     */
   158	    public function testUnmappedFields(): void
   159	    {
   160	        $inputValues = [
   161	            'nameJp' => 'テスト',
   162	            'nameEn' => 'Test',
   163	            'rank' => 1,
   164	            'commanderInMain' => true,
   165	            'commandMaxCardCount' => 5,
   166	            'commandMinCardCount' => 1,
   167	        ];
   168	
   169	        $this->form->submit($inputValues);
   170	
   171	        $this->assertTrue($this->form->isValid());
   172	
   173	        /** @var MtbFormat $Format */
   174	        $Format = $this->form->getData();
   175	
   176	        // mapped=false なので Entity には存在しない
   177	        $this->assertFalse(method_exists($Format, 'getCommandMaxCardCount'));
   178	    }

    69	    public function index(Request $request, PaginatorInterface $paginator, int $page_no = 1): array
    70	    {
    71	        $searchForm = $this->getForm();
    72	
    73	        $event = new EventArgs(
    74	            [
    75	                'form' => $searchForm,
    76	            ],
    77	            $request
    78	        );
    79	        $this->eventDispatcher->dispatch($event, EccubeEvents::ADMIN_CARD_INDEX_INITIALIZE);
    80	
    81	        if ($request->isMethod('POST')) {
    82	            $searchForm->handleRequest($request);
    83	
    84	            if (!$searchForm->isValid()) {
    85	                return [
    86	                    'searchForm' => $searchForm->createView(),
    87	                    'pagination' => null,
    88	                    'is_search_filter_active' => true,
    89	                ];
    90	            }
    91	
    92	            $page_no = 1;
    93	            $viewData = FormUtil::getViewData($searchForm);
    94	
    95	            $this->session->set($this->sessionKey, $viewData);
    96	            $this->session->set($this->sessionKey.'.page_no', $page_no);
    97	        } else {
    98	            $pageNoAttr = $request->attributes->get('page_no');
    99	            if ($request->query->getInt('resume') === Constant::ENABLED || $pageNoAttr !== null) {
   100	                if ($pageNoAttr !== null) {
   101	                    $page_no = (int) $pageNoAttr;
   102	                    $this->session->set($this->sessionKey.'.page_no', $page_no);
   103	                } else {
   104	                    $page_no = $this->session->get($this->sessionKey.'.page_no', 1);
   105	                }
   106	                $viewData = $this->session->get($this->sessionKey, []);
   107	                FormUtil::submitAndGetData($searchForm, $viewData);
   108	            } else {
   109	                $viewData = FormUtil::getViewData($searchForm);
   110	                $this->session->set($this->sessionKey, $viewData);
   111	                $this->session->set($this->sessionKey.'.page_no', 1);
   112	
   113	                return [
   114	                    'searchForm' => $searchForm->createView(),
   115	                    'pagination' => null,
   116	                    'is_search_filter_active' => false,
   117	                ];
   118	            }
   119	        }
   120	
   121	        $responseData = $this->search($request, null, $page_no);
   122	
   123	        // 詳細検索項目に値がある場合のみアコーディオンを開く判定を行う
   124	        $searchData = $searchForm->getData();
   125	        $responseData['is_search_filter_active'] = $this->checkSearchFilterActive($searchData);
   126	
   127	        $responseData['sortKeys'] = $this->cardRepository->getSortKeys();
   128	        $responseData['sortKey'] = $searchData['sortKey'] ?? $this->cardRepository::DEFAULT_SORT_KEY;
   129	        $responseData['sortOrder'] = $searchData['sortOrder'] ?? $this->cardRepository::DEFAULT_SORT_ORDER;
   130	
   131	        return $responseData;
   132	    }
   133	
   134	    /**
   135	     * 詳細検索項目が入力されているかチェックする.
   136	     *
   137	     * @param array<string, mixed> $searchData 検索条件
   138	     *
   139	     * @return bool 詳細検索項目に入力がある場合 true
   140	     */
   141	    private function checkSearchFilterActive(array $searchData): bool
   142	    {
   143	        // 判定から除外する項目（これらのみの場合は詳細検索を閉じさせる）
   144	        $excludeKeys = ['multi', 'sortKey', 'sortOrder'];
   145	
   146	        foreach ($searchData as $key => $value) {
   147	            if (in_array($key, $excludeKeys, true)) {
   148	                continue;
   149	            }
   150	
   151	            if ($value !== null && $value !== '') {
   152	                // 配列（EntityTypeの複数選択など）の場合は中身があるか確認
   153	                if (is_iterable($value)) {
   154	                    foreach ($value as $_) {
   155	                        return true;
   156	                    }
   157	                    continue;
   158	                }
   159	
   160	                return true;
   161	            }
   162	        }
   163	
   164	        return false;
   165	    }

   170	    #[Route('/%eccube_admin_route%/card/generate_list', name: 'admin_card_generate_list', methods: ['POST'])]
   171	    public function generateList(Request $request): JsonResponse
   172	    {
   173	        $tokenValue = $request->request->get('_token');
   174	        $token = new CsrfToken('create_card_name_list', $tokenValue);
   175	        if (!$this->csrfTokenManager->isTokenValid($token)) {
   176	            return new JsonResponse([
   177	                'result' => 'failure',
   178	                'message' => trans('admin.common.csrf_invalid'),
   179	            ]);
   180	        }
   181	
   182	        try {
   183	            $result = $this->cardNameListService->create();
   184	
   185	            $event = new EventArgs([], $request);
   186	            $this->eventDispatcher->dispatch($event, EccubeEvents::ADMIN_CARD_GENERATE_LIST_COMPLETE);
   187	
   188	            return new JsonResponse([
   189	                'result' => ($result[0] === 0) ? 'success' : 'failure',
   190	                'message' => ($result[0] === 0) ? trans('admin.common.save_complete') : $result[1],
   191	            ]);
   192	        } catch (\Exception $e) {
   193	            return new JsonResponse([
   194	                'result' => 'failure',
   195	                'message' => $e->getMessage(),
   196	            ]);
   197	        }
   198	    }
